In the limit $\langle\mathrm{count}\times r/q\rangle \to 1/(2\pi) = 0.159$, which the nine readings straddle.}
\end{table}

\paragraph{Newton's law and the place of $G$.} A body $A$ of content
$M_A$ at the mean flow $\langle a\rangle$ takes per interval the push
$-M_A\langle a\rangle$, and its speed on an axis is $|p_a|/(N_l N_w M_A +
|p_a|)$; for $|p_a| \ll N_l N_w M_A$ the acceleration is
$\mathrm{push}/(N_l N_w M_A)$ Links per interval squared, so
\begin{equation}\label{eq:newton}
a = -\frac{G M_B}{r^2}, \quad G = \frac{K\,\eta}{4\pi N_w}\ \ \text{in space};
\qquad a = -\frac{G' M_B}{r}, \quad G' = \frac{K\,\eta}{2\pi N_w}\ \ \text{on the plane},
\end{equation}
in Links cubed (squared) per unit of content per interval squared: $G$
is the source's release rate per unit of content per direction, $\eta$,
times the number of directions $K$, over $4\pi$ and the world's width
$N_w$. The registered check is the plane's $1/r$ (Table~\ref{tab:rings});
the $1/r^2$ of space is the same shell mean over $4\pi r^2$, not run.
Nothing of $A$ enters: the equivalence principle is exact (registered:
$\mathrm{push}_m = m \times \mathrm{push}_1$ for $m = 4, 16$ against the
probe of content $1$, at $180$ records and three axes, and the step rule
divides by $M_A$). The third law at rest is exact to the grain of the
apportioning (registered: the two sources' momenta $9612145197056$ and
$-9612088573952$, the ratio $1.0000$). Two departures from Newton's law
as written, both stated: the field is retarded at $c$ (registered: the
front at $r$ arrives at the interval $1$ plus the flight table's first
age at $r$ Links on the heading, $8, 11, 14, 21, 28, 35, 42, 52, 69$ at
$r = 4$ to $40$), so the limit is the retarded inverse square and not
action at a distance; and the speed the push builds saturates at one
Link per interval, above the rows' pace, so a body can outrun its own
field's rows (shown by the test fixture of the derivation's Doppler
section, a body at $0.75$ Links per interval, not by a registered world;
the cap at $c$ is the decided drive, in build and not on main). Reached in the shell
mean, $G$ named; $G$ is a formula of the release rate, the fan and the
width, and the width $N_w$ is an input, a choice of units
(Table~\ref{tab:consequences}).

\paragraph{Coulomb's law and the one constant.} The charge column adds
$+\rho_A\rho_B M_A \mathbf a$ per axis. With the charges $q_A =
\rho_A M_A$ and $q_B = \rho_B M_B$ and $\langle a\rangle$ proportional
to $M_B$ as above, the electric push in the shell mean is
\begin{equation}\label{eq:coulomb}
\mathrm{push}_e = q_A q_B\,\frac{K\eta\,N_l}{4\pi r^2}, \qquad
a_e = \frac{q_A q_B}{M_A}\,\frac{G}{r^2},
\end{equation}
Coulomb's inverse square with the same constant as gravity, $k_C = G$ in
the law's units (charge in units of content), repulsive for like signs
since $\mathbf a$ points away from the source. The ratio of the two
forces on one body is $-\rho_A\rho_B = -q_A q_B/(M_A M_B)$ at every $r$.
Registered: the electric over the gravitational push $-1$ and $-1/4$
exactly (the probes of charge $[2, 1]$ on content $1$ and $[1, 2]$ on
content $4$ against the source's $[1, 2]$ on $2^{24}$), and the
coefficients $M_A(\rho_A\rho_B - 1)$ read integer by integer on the
records (the source of content $2^{24}$ reading the probe's rows, and
the probe reading the source's): $0$ and $0$ for like charges with
$\rho_A\rho_B = 1$ (the electric push cancels gravity exactly), $-2^{25}$
and $-2$ at $\rho_A\rho_B = -1$, $-(3/4)\,2^{24}$ and $-3$ at $1/4$,
$-2^{24}$ and $-1$ for the uncharged probe. Reached in form, the ratio
exact at $r = 12$. Nature's ratio for two protons, $e^2/(4\pi\epsilon_0 G
m_p^2) = 1.24 \times 10^{36}$ ($e$ the elementary charge, $\epsilon_0$
the vacuum permittivity, $m_p$ the proton's mass), is $\rho_p^2$ here,
$\rho_p = 1.1 \times 10^{18}$ units of charge per unit of content: the
hierarchy is the declared $\rho$, an input, not a formula of the law.

\paragraph{What departs, and where.} The field along a beam does not
fall, and off it is zero; the shell's Node count against $2\pi r$ and
$4\pi r^2$; a cube fan's corner-to-face density, the declaration's; the
flow's $|\mathbf u_D|$ within $1.35$ percent of $N_l$, the integer square
root at load, vanishing as $N_l$ grows; the field retarded at $c$ and the
body's speed capped above it; the clock's count under the presence word,
the engine's default, which on a beam does not fall with $r$ (the
potential's $1/r$ is the age moment's reading, the clock's word by P9, the delay field's row of Table~\ref{tab:consequences},
the derivation's 5.1 and series E); and the charge column's floor per
interval where $\rho_A\rho_B$ is not whole, exact in the sum over a
period only under an accumulator that is decided and not in the
archived code. Each is a computed departure, with its size where it has
one, none a fit. In the precedents' form of words \cite{precedents}:
Newton's law of attraction and Coulomb's are what the coupling reduces
to at first order in the Link over $r$, read in series C's integers;
the section confronts no number of nature (the protons' ratio it names
enters as an input), since $G$ carries
the declared width (an input) and no registered orbit confirms a period
against an astronomer's, and the departures above are its admitted gap.

\section{The delay field: Poisson's equation, the retarded wave equation and the clock's redshift}\label{sec:delay}

The rows of Table~\ref{tab:consequences} on the delay field are written
out here from the derivation's section 5 \cite{derivations} and the
register's series E \cite{register}: an open cube of $31^3$ Nodes, a
fixed source of content $2^{12}$ at the centre releasing one row per
self-creation on the $290$ primitive directions with $|a| + |b| + |c|
\le 6$ ($q = 290$ per interval), probes of content $1$ that read and do
not push at every Node of the shells $r = 4$ to $14$ ($6984$ probes), two
worlds of $300$ intervals, the readings pinned before the runs, the
fingerprint in the register; and, for the bending, series K.

\paragraph{What is delayed.} Nothing in flight is delayed: a row moves
by the flight table at one pace, blind to the crowd (series K,
registered: the mean age $89.40$ in every world, the difference $0.00$).
What a crowd delays is a clock. A body reads at its Node the presence
of every row of another number (the engine's default) or, on an entry
that reads the age, their age moment (the clock's word by P9, the
registered clock worlds below reading it so) and owes after each self-creation the whole part of $a_r\,n_c/d_c$
intervals before the next, $a_r$ what it read and $[n_c, d_c]$ the clock's
pair of Part~I ($[1, 1]$ in the scalar world, $[1, 2]$ in the age
world), so that its rate is $1/(1 + a_r\,n_c/d_c)$; its push reads the
flow of the same rows. The operations: the
release and the walk (translations), the reading (the zeroth moment, the
age moment, the first moment: bilinear forms), the owed count (the
Euclidean division), the coupling (a bilinear form).

\paragraph{The two fields of one stream.} Let a source release $q(t)$
rows per interval on a fan of every direction (the limit of
Section~\ref{sec:newton}'s shell mean), each row moving at $c$ and
carrying its age. The presence $\Pi$ at the distance $r = |\mathbf x|$
from the source at the time $t$ is the rows dwelling there, and the age
moment $\mathcal A$ is the presence times the age they carry, $r/c$:
\begin{equation}\label{eq:fields}
\Pi(\mathbf x, t) = \frac{q(t - r/c)\,\tau_L}{4\pi r^2}, \qquad
\mathcal A(\mathbf x, t) = \frac{q(t - r/c)\,\tau_L}{4\pi c\,r},
\end{equation}
$\tau_L$ the radial residence factor, the intervals a row spends per
unit of Euclidean distance, $T_D/(N_l|D|) = 1/v_D$ ($1.72$ on a
heading, $\sqrt3$ in the limit of every direction); the intervals per
lattice Link along a row's staircase are $T_D/(S_1 N_l)$, a different
factor ($1$ on a body diagonal), and a finite shell's coefficient
depends on its digital geometry, so the coefficient of
\eqref{eq:fields} is the heading's on a beam and the limit's on the
sphere. The presence is the retarded flux of a conserved
stream; the age moment is the retarded potential of the source, since
$f(t - r/c)/(4\pi r)$ is the retarded Green's function of the wave
operator, so $\mathcal A$ obeys the inhomogeneous scalar wave equation
with the release as its source,
\begin{equation}\label{eq:wave}
\frac{1}{c^2}\,\frac{\partial^2 \mathcal A}{\partial t^2} - \nabla^2 \mathcal A = \frac{\tau_L}{c}\,q(t)\,\delta^3(\mathbf x),
\end{equation}
$\delta^3$ the three-dimensional Dirac delta, and in the static limit
Poisson's equation $\nabla^2\mathcal A = -(\tau_L/c)\,q\,\delta^3(\mathbf
x)$, solved by $\mathcal A = (\tau_L q/4\pi c)/r$. The moments are sums
over rows, so the field of many sources is the sum of their fields: the
theory is linear. The flow the push reads is the stream's flux vector,
$\mathbf a = (qN_l/4\pi r^2)\,\hat{\mathbf r}$, $\hat{\mathbf r}$ the
unit vector from the source and $N_l$ the label each unit carries, which
is $-(N_l c/\tau_L)\,\nabla \mathcal A$ in the static case: the
acceleration of a body is minus the gradient of the age moment's field.
Reached: in the limit of every direction the age moment is the retarded
potential of the release and obeys the wave equation there; on the GameBoard, the shell means of series E. Registered, with $a_r$ and $a_\tau$
the presence and the age moment a probe reads (the register's \texttt{k}, scalar
and age): $a_r r^2 = 41.5$ over $r = 6$ to $14$ ($39.0$ to
$44.8$; the design's $q\tau_L/4\pi = 290 \times 1.72/12.57 = 39.7$,
pinned as $40$ with the criterion a constant within fifteen percent of
its mean) and $a_\tau r = 36.1$ ($33.1$ to $39.2$), their ratio $0.870$
against the pinned $\sqrt3/2 = 0.866$ (the age at $r$ is $r\sqrt3$
intervals, the age world's pair $[1, 2]$ halving it): the presence as
$M/r^2$ and the age moment as $M/r$ from the same rows, $M$ the source's
content, with the shell's ripple as in Section~\ref{sec:newton}.

\paragraph{The clock's redshift.} A clock at the age moment $a_\tau =
C_a M/r$, $C_a$ the constant of Eq.~\eqref{eq:fields} times the clock's
pair, runs at $1/(1 + a_\tau)$; general relativity's clock at the potential runs
at $\sqrt{1 - 2GM/rc^2} = 1 - GM/rc^2 - \tfrac12 (GM/rc^2)^2 - \dots$.
Under the calibration $a_\tau = GM/rc^2$, assumed (the clock's pair and the width do not fix it), the two agree at first order: the redshift between
two clocks, $1 - a_r(r_1) + a_r(r_2)$, is the $1/r$ law. Reached at first
order. At second order the GameBoard reads $1 - a_r + a_r^2$ against $1 - a_r -
a_r^2/2$, and its rate never reaches zero at a finite $a_r$: a different law
in the strong field, no horizon (the crowd only slows; nothing stops a
clock). Registered: the rate ratios $\mathrm{rate}(r)/\mathrm{rate}(14)
= 0.3453, 0.5015, 0.5701, 0.7227, 0.8215$ at $r = 4$ to $12$ against
the $1/r$ law's $0.3627, 0.5162, 0.6548, 0.7805, 0.8951$ with $a_\tau r$
fitted at $r = 14$ ($33.12$; with the shell mean $36.07$ the list is
$0.3570, 0.5101, 0.6492, 0.7763, 0.8928$): the form is the law's (the
nearer clock slower, the shift $1/r - 1/14$), inside the pinned band at
$r = 6$ and outside at $8, 10, 12$ by the shell mean's ripple entering
the ratio whole, at a strong field ($a_\tau = 2$ to $9$) where the
first-order line fails as pinned; the weak-field confrontation ($a_\tau \ll
1$) is a run the register names as the follow-up, neither pinned nor
made. The clock alone, without the field's push, is read on the tree's
crowd-clock worlds (series U in the clock note's lettering
\cite{clockage}, its retitling pending; \cite{crowdclock}): a lamp inside a crowd of two
sources whose rows it lets pass, the coupling off by the world's word,
read by a fixed detector; eight worlds whose expectation the design
pinned before any run, run twice on main (the commit in
Reproducibility; the first
run read the lamp's own spending, its wheel turning by its content,
and the worlds were amended to a reservoir of $2^{20}$ units), the
entry drafted and not registered. Every one of the $32$ readings of
the second run inside its pin: a lamp at rest reads $1 + z = 1 + a_r$
with $a_r = 4F/2^{16}$, $F$ the crowd's units per source per interval,
inside its pin at every rung, $1.000$ and $1.006$ in the two windows
for the pinned $1.005$ at $a_r = 0.005$ (inside by the pin's tolerance
of $0.02$, the whole of $a_r$ there), and exact at $a_r = 0.08, 0.3, 1, 2$
($1 + z = 1.080, 1.300, 2.000, 3.000$), so $z = 1$ and $z = 2$ are
read with nothing moving and the count is unbounded; every birth reaches the
detector; a lamp thrown at $0.2c$ inside its crowd reads $1.283$,
between the sum $1 + a_r + v/c$ and the product $(1 + a_r)(1 + v/c)$ and
nearer the sum, and, stepping only at a self-creation, moves at $v/(1
+ a_r)$ and falls behind its unslowed crowd within the pinned bracket
(record 359 \cite{log}); measured once (the $a_r$ of these worlds is the
presence at the lamp's Node, the crowd's rows there, where the clock
of the paragraph above reads the age moment). Five
such members before one detector (series V, its retitling pending), at rest and thrown as one at $0.2c$ with each member's sources
at $v/(1 + a_r)$, the design pinned before its one run on main (the commit in
Reproducibility), the entry drafted and not registered
\cite{crowdclock}: at rest $1 + z = 1.0000, 1.0999, 1.3001, 1.6000,
2.0000$ at $a_r = 0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1$, a spread of $0.363$ in $z$ with
nothing moving and no member below the bare one's $1$; thrown as one
$1.2007, 1.3022, 1.5066, 1.8092, 2.2147$ for the pinned $1 + a_r + v/c$,
the terms adding, the product $(1 + a_r)(1 + v/c)$ refuted from $a_r = 0.3$
up ($2.4$ at $a_r = 1$); measured once. A reader inside a crowd of its
own (series S), $a_r'$, with a lamp of its own for its clock, reading a source
inside a crowd $a_r$ (five worlds, one run on the same commit, the
entry drafted and not registered \cite{crowdclock}): in the reader's
own clock $1 + z = (1 + a_r + v/c)/(1 + a_r')$, the sum form over the
reader's own factor, $0.5000$ for a reader
denser than its source, $1.0000$ for crowds alike, $1.1054$ for the
receding source's pinned $1.100$, ten of ten readings inside; measured
once. Which of the two words the clock counts, the presence or the age
moment, was put to the GameBoard as series T of the register, the
physicist's pins written before the run \cite{register,clockage}: a
lamp at rest, two sources of $F = 4915$ units per interval each at $3$
and then at $6$ Links, the detector at $x = 110$; under the presence
word the detector reads $1 + z = 1.3000$ at both distances, the
presence clock unable to tell them apart; under the age word $2.6517$
at $3$ and $4.1500$ at $6$, the ratio of the two $a_\tau$ being $1.907$
for the pinned $1.909$ (the continuum's potential $2.000$, outside the
pin as the lattice's dwelling ages say); every pin met; measured once.
In the precedents' form of words: the law as declared reads either
word per table entry; under the age word the clock's shift follows the
potential's form, the physicist's map putting the GPS
ground-to-orbit shift at $45.7$ microseconds per day against Ashby's
$45.7$ \cite{ashby2003}, where the presence word gives $28.3$. The
owner's word of 2026-09-21 chose the age moment as the clock's word,
entered as an assumption of the law (P9) and not as a derivation,
where the law as built counts the presence by default and the age
moment on an entry that reads the age, the default to follow the word;
the presence word stays in the record as the alternative refuted on
the form; the second runner's round is pending. What the runs cannot say is
whether a galaxy's redshift is such a clock or a cluster's dispersion a
spread of such clocks; they say what the law as built makes of a
crowd.

\paragraph{Bodies: Newton's geodesics, retarded.} A body's push reads
the flow and its speed the step rule, so in the limit it follows the
geodesics of the Newtonian potential $A$, retarded at $c$, and not of a
metric: no term of order $v^2/c^2$ or $(GM/rc^2)^2$ enters the push (the
coupling is bilinear in the flow and the content and nothing else), so
the perihelion advance has no source here. Reached for Newton's motion;
not reached for the post-Newtonian terms. No registered orbit confirms
or refutes it: series D closes no orbit, and its flat-curve period ratio
$3.73$ against $4$ is the six-heading shell structure of
Section~\ref{sec:newton}, not a precession.

\paragraph{Light: no optical metric.} A row reads nothing of the crowd:
beside a mass it is neither bent nor delayed, exactly (series K, three
worlds: the deflection $0.000$ pixel at the content and impact distance
$(2^{12}, 6)$, $(2^{13}, 6)$ and $(2^{12}, 3)$, at a crowd where nature
would capture the beam). Not reached: the equivalence principle holds
for bodies and not for rows; the delay field is a metric for clocks and
for the bodies' pushes, not for the flight. Under the world key
\texttt{meeting}, off by default (a paid row turning toward the crowd by
one step $\Delta\theta$ of the direction table per $\Nphi$ crowd units met),
the deflection is $q\,\Delta\theta/(4\Nphi bc)$, the form $M/b$ of Newton's
and Einstein's with the sign toward the mass and the constant a grain of
the fan, and the phase delay reads the flow ($1/b$) where the Shapiro
delay reads the potential (a logarithm): reached in form for the
bending, a different law for the delay, and no delay in time under
either. Registered under the key: the centroid toward the mass in every
world, $-1.79$, $-4.36$, $-2.30$ pixels against the offline flight's
$-3.0$, $-4.3$, $-2.6$, the first outside because $122$ of the most
turned rows clicked on the mass itself; the smallest step of that table
is $2.4$ degrees, $10^4$ times nature's $1.75$ arcseconds, so the value
is out of reach by the grain and is not claimed.

\paragraph{The general flux, and what Einstein's equation has that this
does not.} The exact statement the GameBoard has is Gauss's law of the
stream at every instant: in the continuum $\nabla\cdot\mathbf g = -4\pi
G\rho$, $\mathbf g = -\nabla\mathcal A$ the field, $\rho$ the content
density and $G$ Newton's constant of Eq.~\eqref{eq:newton}, and
dynamically the continuity
equation of the rows (the books hold the content exactly). This is the
flux form of the field equation, exact on the GameBoard and linear in the
sources. Einstein's equation reduces to it in the weak static limit and
has beyond it: a tensor source (pressure and momentum flux gravitate;
here only the content's release does); the field's own energy as a
source (here the rows carry content $0$, are read by no row, and the
fields of two sources add exactly: gravity does not gravitate); the
metric acting on light and on clocks alike (here on clocks and on the
bodies' pushes, not on the flight); and a cosmological term (here none:
series G2's deceleration $+0.922$ under the step drive and the record
click and $+0.345$ under the reading's weight at the relative speed, a
key since deleted, with gravity on, $-0.108$ coasting, the law having no
term that gives $q < 0$; Table~\ref{tab:nature}, row 3). Not reached for
Einstein's equation; reached for its weak-field flux form and its
retardation. Where a rule for the general formulas would live is named
\cite{log}: on the linear block, at one Node, a row's pace or turn read
off the rows present at its own Node; the derivation's Einstein map
lists each result with what must be added under which identity
(\cite{derivations}, 21.4),
and the design of that rule, optical-v1, is reviewed twice, buildable
and not yet built \cite{grrows}. In the precedents' form of words: the
clock's redshift at first order is equivalent to the potential's, which
Delva et al.\ read to $2.5 \times 10^{-5}$ of the
effect \cite{delva2018} after Vessot et al.\ \cite{vessot1980}; the
law's second order, $1 - a_r + a_r^2$ against $1 - a_r - a_r^2/2$, lies at
$5 \times 10^{-19}$ at the Earth, below reach (Table~\ref{tab:differs},
row 12), and the weak-field run not made is the gap admitted.

\section{The dark sector: a negative result, with the missing content located}\label{sec:dark}

This section reports what was tried on the law toward the flat rotation
curves and the accelerated expansion, and what refuted each attempt. It
explains neither; it says where the law puts the content it does not
have \cite{darksector,sanda0,farlamp}.

\paragraph{The statement, with its one-line reason.} In the weak field the
law's gravity is Newton's in the shell mean, with Gauss's law exact
(Section~\ref{sec:newton}): the push is bilinear in the flow and the
content and homogeneous of degree $1$ in the arriving rows (a reading of
its form, $M_A(\rho_A\rho_B - 1)\mathbf a$ with $\mathbf a$ linear in
the rows), and the gravity column's division has the divisor $1$, so
nothing is discarded (the charge column's remainder is kept by the
accumulator). Hence, as the notes argue and no source proves as a
theorem, no rule of the six operations strengthens a weak field, and
none gives an acceleration below which the field departs from $1/r^2$.
What nature measures at a galaxy's edge is such a departure: the flat
curves set in at one acceleration $a_0 = 1.2 \times 10^{-10}$ m
s$^{-2}$ and not at one radius \cite{milgrom1983,mcgaugh2016}, and the
flat speed follows the visible content as $v^4 \propto M$ (the baryonic
Tully--Fisher relation, slope about $4$: $3.9 \pm 0.2$ \cite{mcgaugh2012},
$3.85 \pm 0.09$ \cite{lelli2019}).

\paragraph{The falsifiable rows.} In one list: the far lamp's brightness,
$q_{\mathrm{eff}} = +1$ against $-0.53$, FAIL (Table~\ref{tab:nature},
row 11a); the stream's stretch $1 + z$, PASS (row 11b); the surface
brightness $(1 + z)^{-1}$ against $(1 + z)^{-4}$, FAIL (row 11c); and
the rotation curve of a disc galaxy, which the law predicts Keplerian
with the visible content (the shell mean of Section~\ref{sec:newton},
read over many stars at many angles), FAIL against the flat curves:
this paper's own reading, no register row.

\paragraph{What was tried, and what refuted it.} Six routes, each read
on the law's own arithmetic in the three notes cited, none a rule, none
run; Table~\ref{tab:dark} gives each with the sign and the shape it
gives.

\begin{table}[H]\centering\footnotesize
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
\begin{tabular}{p{1.6in}p{2.4in}p{2.2in}}
\toprule
The route & What it gives & What refutes it \\
\midrule
The reading through a detector: the edge's speed as the law's Doppler & the receiver's $1 \pm v/c$, the source's $1/(1 \mp v/c)$, the moving reader's $1 - \mathbf n \cdot \boldsymbol\beta$ ($\mathbf n$ the arriving row's direction, $\boldsymbol\beta$ the reader's velocity over $c$); at $\beta = 3$ to $7 \times 10^{-4}$ every departure from the classical Doppler is of order $\beta^2$, parts in $10^{4}$ of the shift & the curves are read to a part in $100$ of the shift; no reading through a detector makes a Keplerian edge look flat \\
The far system read before any click & the arrival count, which no spread of the unclicked offers' labels or phases moves; a spread fixed at birth, or growing with the distance or the crowd met & the Solar System's planets read from the same detector at $1$ m s$^{-1}$ \cite{mayor2003}, a part in $2 \times 10^{5}$ of the edge speed; Tully--Fisher's independence of distance; a cluster's dispersion is of centres, not widths \\
A short periodic dimension (a slab whose third axis is periodic with the thickness $\ell_{\mathrm{slab}}$) & a flat curve indeed: beyond $\ell_{\mathrm{slab}}$ the shell is a cylinder and the mean falls as $1/r$ (the counted exponent $-0.7$ to $-1.0$ against $-1.8$ to $-2.1$ on the open GameBoard) & the Tully--Fisher slope $2$ against $4$ (a dwarf of $10^{9}$ solar masses at $22$ km s$^{-1}$ against nature's $68$); one length for every source where nature has one acceleration; a repeated sky at $1$ to $10$ kpc \\
The width $N_w$ derived, so that $G$ follows & $N_w = K\eta/(4\pi)$ (a choice of units), $N_w$ from the Planck mass (refuted at once, $\alpha_G \ge 1$ against $5.9 \times 10^{-39}$), $N_w = K_{\mathrm{budget}}/26$ (the budget reading), $N_w$ from a computation budget tied to the wall's rate $H$ & a unit choice moves the input into $h$; the budget's constant is unfixed; $G$ running with $H$ is refuted by lunar laser ranging, $\dot G/G = (7.1 \pm 7.6) \times 10^{-14}$ per year \cite{hofmann2018}, by a factor $900$ ($2800$ in the cubic form) \\
The push's rounding as an acceleration floor & the divisions keep the remainder: the floor is zero; a discarding floor cuts the weak field in a band & the wrong sign and the wrong shape against the radial acceleration relation; the deep law a flat curve needs, $a = \sqrt{a_N a_0}$, is a root of the state, not one of the six \\
The fixed computation per Node per interval, $K_c$ (the derivation's section 13 constant), at a galaxy's edge & (1) a cap on the release weakens the far field where it acts, keeps the $1/r^2$ shape and acts on no galaxy; (2) a cap on the forwarding conserves the flux by the books and leaves Newton at the edge; (3) a floor by comparison: the budget spent on reading the edge's crowd is $10^{-5}$ of an interval at $r = 100$ Links for $K_c = 10^{4}$ & (1) the wrong direction; (2) no change at the edge; (3) no operation makes a sparse Node push more than it reads, and a floor by comparison rises as $r$ \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\caption{\label{tab:dark}What was tried toward the flat rotation curves on the law's own arithmetic, and what refuted it \cite{darksector,sanda0}. Every number of the law's is the host map's, the pins are nature's, cited; no run was made, since no pin was met on paper. $\ell_{\mathrm{slab}}$ is the slab's thickness, $\alpha_G$ nature's gravitational coupling of the proton, $a_N$ Newton's acceleration and $a_0$ nature's scale, $K_c$ the fixed computation per Node per interval.}
\end{table}

\paragraph{Where the missing content sits.} The law does not remove the
need for unseen content; it locates it. Dark matter enters as an input
family of the table (a charge column $0$, no lamp: it pushes and is
pushed by the width $N_w$ like every other content and releases no row a
detector reads), exactly as it enters the standard model of cosmology,
and nothing in the six operations forbids or derives it; its amount per
galaxy is an initial state. Dark energy enters nowhere: the growing wall
is coasting, and after a detector the law's lamp is brighter than
nature's by one factor of $1 + z$. The bending of rows, on the archived
code, is zero (series K), and under the meeting key it follows the
content met, so a bending mass where no visible content is (the Bullet
Cluster \cite{clowe2006}) needs unseen content there, as nature's model
says. At the time of writing the law has no explanation of the flat
rotation curves.

\paragraph{The open question, in one line.} A rule that reproduces the
radial acceleration relation requires a root of the state, $a =
\sqrt{a_N a_0}$, and a declared $a_0$; it is not one of the six
operations and fails the vector test, so it would enter only under its
own identity, outside the law, and would still put the Bullet Cluster's
bending on the gas.

\section{The click's square as a positive quadratic form forced by the splitter: a lattice Gleason}\label{sec:gleason}

The row of Table~\ref{tab:consequences} on the click's weight is written
out here from the derivation's section 6.5 \cite{derivations}; the
register's checks are the click chapter's own (series L, Part~III). The
click's square, the norm of a record's summed pointer, is the one
operation of the law beyond the translation, the wall, the declared
matrix and the addition, and the place where the Born rule enters.
Asked: whether the operations of Part~I, with one hypothesis on the
reading, force the reading at a click to be that square, or what weaker
statement holds and what the register pins. (The derivation's ``Theorem
2'' of the click paper is Theorem~\ref{th:isometry} here; the theorems
are numbered through the manuscript.)

\paragraph{The objects.} A record's rows at one Node and label are one
element $f = \sum_p f_p x^p$ of the group ring $\Z[\Z_{\Nphi}]$, $x$ the
generator of one phase step and $f_p$ the amount at the phase $p$; the
pointer is $\mathrm{ev}(f) = \sum_p f_p
\zeta^p$, $\zeta$ the primitive $\Nphi$-th root of unity, at the tables'
scale. The merge's cancel identifies $x^{\Nphi/2}$ with $-1$, so the rows
after the cancel are an element of $\Z[x]/(x^{\Nphi/2} + 1)$; for $\Nphi$ a
power of two (every registered $\Nphi$) that quotient is the ring of
cyclotomic integers $\Z[\zeta_{\Nphi}]$ itself, a free $\Z$-module of rank
$\Nphi/2$, and $\mathrm{ev}$ is the identity on it: the GameBoard's cancel
and the click's zero are one relation, exactly (for an $\Nphi$ with an odd
factor they are not; the register has no such $\Nphi$). The reading is a
function $R: \Z[\zeta_{\Nphi}] \to \mathbb R$ of a cell's element, up to one
factor per record, which the multiplicity $\mathtt m$ books.

\paragraph{The hypotheses.} (a) The phase rotation, $R(xf) = R(f)$: a
rotation of the record's phase by one step changes no weight (the rungs
take no $u$; $u$ only selects among them), an operation of Part~I. (b)
The balanced splitter conserves the reading for every two inputs,
$R(x^{\Nphi/4} f + g) + R(f + x^{\Nphi/4} g) = A_2\,(R(f) + R(g))$ for all $f,
g$ (the $(1, 1)$ split with the quarter turn on the reflected row, $A_2$
its multiplicity factor): a hypothesis on the reading, stronger than
Part~I's split, which is an integer matrix on rows and says nothing of
what a detector reads under it; the register checks it only on the
registered pairs (the totals $64/0$ and $32/32$ of Part~III). (c)
Counts, $R(f) \ge 0$. (d) The empty reads zero: $R(0) = 0$ on the zero
count vector. (e) Some input reads: $R$ is not identically zero.
Without (d) the hypotheses admit a detector that always reads $1$,
which satisfies (a), (b) with $A_2 = 1$ and (c) and is no quadratic
form; without (e) they admit the zero detector, under which (b) holds
for every $A_2$; so (a) to (c) alone do not force the square. (d) and
(e) are what the engine does (the Gram form on the empty record is
$0$; a click needs a live unit) and what the proof's first step uses
(the model owner's finding, record 340 \cite{log}; the derivation's
6.5 restated with them). Not assumed: continuity, a dimension, the form of $R$ on
one row, or Theorem~\ref{th:isometry}'s $A = \sum a_i^2$.

\begin{theorem}[The lattice Gleason]\label{th:gleason}
Under (a) to (e), with $\Nphi$ a power of two, $A_2 = 2$ and
\begin{equation}\label{eq:gleason}
R(f) = \sum_{j\ \mathrm{odd},\ 1 \le j < \Nphi/2} c_j\,|\sigma_j(f)|^2, \qquad c_j \ge 0,
\qquad \sigma_j(f) = \sum_p f_p\,e^{2\pi i j p/\Nphi},
\end{equation}
the $\sigma_j$ the Galois conjugates of $\mathrm{ev}$, $\sigma_1 =
\mathrm{ev}$. Every such $R$ is a positive quadratic form on the lattice,
homogeneous, $R(af) = a^2 R(f)$; it conserves the total at every
declared split of one row or of two recombining inputs exactly if and
only if the multiplicity factor is $A = \sum a_i^2$, and at every label
rotation with the factor $C'^2 + S'^2$, $C'$ and $S'$ the half-angle
tables of Part~III. Two rows $ax^p$ and $bx^{p+\Delta}$ at one Node,
$\Delta$ their phase difference in steps, read $R = C_\Sigma(a^2 + b^2)
+ 2ab\,\mathcal K(\Delta)$ with $C_\Sigma = \sum c_j$ and
$\mathcal K(\Delta) = \sum c_j \cos(2\pi j\Delta/\Nphi)$, $\mathcal K(0) =
C_\Sigma$, $\mathcal K(\Nphi/4) = 0$, $\mathcal K(\Nphi/2) = -C_\Sigma$ for every
admissible $R$; the Born form $|\mathrm{ev}(f)|^2$ is $c_1 = 1$ and the
other $c_j = 0$.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
Sketch; the derivation's 6.5 \cite{derivations} gives the steps in
full under (a) to (e). (i) $g = 0$ in (b) with (a) gives $2R(f) =
A_2\,(R(f) + R(0)) = A_2 R(f)$ by (d), so $A_2 = 2$, one $f$ with $R(f)
> 0$ sufficing, and (e) supplies it. (ii) Replacing $g$ by $x^{-\Nphi/4}g$ in (b) and
using (a) and $x^{-\Nphi/2} = -1$ gives $R(f - g) + R(f + g) = 2R(f) +
2R(g)$, the parallelogram law on the abelian group $\Z[\zeta_{\Nphi}]$. (iii)
The parallelogram law alone makes $R$ a quadratic form: on a lattice the
argument of Jordan and von Neumann \cite{jordan1935} needs no
continuity, and $R(f) = B(f, f)/2$ with $B$ a $\Z$-bilinear symmetric
form, the restriction of the real form of a symmetric matrix $\mathbf
G$, the click's Gram matrix (not the fan's grain $N_\theta$ of Part~I). (iv) By (a), $\mathbf G$ commutes with the signed permutation of
the basis that multiplication by $x$ is, whose eigen-functionals are the
$\sigma_j$, $j$ odd; so $\mathbf G$ is diagonal in that basis with real
entries paired under $j \leftrightarrow -j$. (v) A negative $c_j$ would
make the form negative on an open cone containing lattice points,
against (c). (vi) The conservation at a general split and at a label
rotation follows from homogeneity, (a) and the cancellation of the
cross terms between the two outputs. (vii) Two rows: $|\sigma_j(a +
bx^\Delta)|^2 = a^2 + b^2 + 2ab\cos(2\pi j\Delta/\Nphi)$.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{The uncertainty relation, from the same evaluation.} A record
is the element $f$ of $\Z[\Z_{\Nphi}]$ of the amounts that ended at each
phase; its position is the Node its rows ended at, its momentum the
label whose turn per Link is $|p|\Nphi/h$ (de Broglie's $\lambda = h/|p|$
Links as the declared turn). The click evaluates $f$ at the $\Nphi$th roots
of unity, which is the discrete Fourier transform on the circle, so
position and phase are the two sides of one transform, and three
statements follow from the transform alone, with no hypothesis
(\cite{derivations}, section 22). The support bound: a record whose
rows end on $s$ phases has an evaluation supported on at least $\Nphi/s$
roots, $|\mathrm{supp}\,f|\,|\mathrm{supp}\,\hat f| \ge \Nphi$
\cite{donoho1989}, checked by enumeration at $\Nphi = 64$ on the register's
records (one phase gives all $64$ roots, the antiphase pair $32$, a comb
of every eighth phase $8$, the equality case). The entropic bound: the
two Shannon entropies sum to at least $\log_2 \Nphi$ \cite{maassen1988},
of the same form as the identity of Section~\ref{sec:table}'s entropy
row, bits read plus bits not read of $u$ ($H(u \mid K)$ for the outcome $K$) $= \log_2 \Nphi$ per record, the outcome's coarse graining ($u$ stays in the world's list as a GameBoard diagnostic), read for the
conjugate pair. The algebra: the Weyl relation of the shift and the phase
multiplication holds exactly, the finite commutator in place of
$i\hbar$. Kennard's variance form $\Delta x\,\Delta p \ge \hbar/2$
\cite{kennard1927,robertson1929} follows, as is well known, by the Cauchy--Schwarz argument
on the Gram form of Theorem~\ref{th:gleason} in the limit $\Nphi \to
\infty$ of a record spread over many phases, with $\hbar = h\Nphi/(2\pi)$;
at finite $\Nphi$ the bound is the finite group's and the Gaussian equality
case does not exist on $\Z_{\Nphi}$. What makes it an uncertainty and not a
spread is the one read-out: a record read at a Node gives its position
and erases its phase vector, a record read on the fan's angle gives its
momentum's direction and not the Node it came from, and the two are not
jointly readable for the same reason the law has no joint distribution
for Bell (Theorem~\ref{th:bell}). The pin for the single opening, from
the transform of a run of $w$ phases times the fan's angular density:
$w\,\Delta(\sin\theta)/\Lambda = 0.886$ in the far field, $0.916$ by
the exact sum at the registered geometry (Fresnel number $1.45$),
written before the run as $0.92 \pm 0.03$ at the width $27$ and $0.886
\pm 0.03$ at the width $9$ (Table~\ref{tab:nature}, row 10, not yet
run).

\paragraph{What is reached and what is not.} Reached: the reading is a
positive quadratic form of the record's element, forced by the rotation,
the balanced splitter's conservation and the counts alone, with no
continuity and no dimension (Gleason's theorem \cite{gleason1957} needs
dimension three and fails at two, where frame functions exist that are
not quadratic; the lattice version does not, because (b) holds for every
two inputs and not only for orthogonal decompositions, which is the
parallelogram law, a stronger hypothesis the balanced splitter grants).
The multiplicity rule $A = \sum a_i^2$ is derived here, not assumed; the
square is the only power ($R(af) = a^2R(f)$: no $|w|^k$ with $k \ne 2$
conserves); and the cross term $2ab\,\mathcal K(\Delta)$, a product
between two rows the GameBoard never forms, is forced to exist at the
click, because the two outputs of a balanced splitter conserve the total
only through cross terms that cancel between them. Not reached by the
algebra: the constants $c_j$, the detector's response to the harmonics
of the phase, the exact analogue of Gleason's free state: a free positive
operator commuting with the rotation, diagonal on the Galois planes. A
single harmonic $j$ alone is the Born form under the relabelling $p \to
jp$ of the phase steps, a change of the declaration and not of the law;
a mixture is a different law, the flat mixture being the phase-blind
detector $R(f) = \sum_{p < \Nphi/2} f_p^2$ on the reduced basis $1, x,
\dots, x^{\Nphi/2-1}$, which satisfies (a), (b), (c) exactly.
The algebra admits the phase-blind detector; what excludes it is the
register.

\paragraph{Against the registered integers.} The power: the $(3, 4)$
split's $63/1$ (Part~III) holds if and only if $7^k$ lies in $[125/3,
127)$, $k \in [1.917, 2.489)$; the far pair's cells $27, 5, 5, 27$ if and
only if $k \in [1.784, 2.054)$; both windows contain $2$ and exclude $1$
and $3$ ($k = 1$ gives $56/8$ and $23, 9, 9, 23$; $k = 3$ gives $64/0$
and $30, 2, 2, 30$). The same split scaled to $\Nphi = 32$ and $\Nphi = 128$,
pinned from the engine's tables and ladder before the run and met
exactly ($31/1$ and $125/3$; the amplitude register's
\texttt{mz\_345\_n} block, record 363 \cite{log}), gives $k \in
[1.548, 2.129)$ and $k \in [1.835, 2.012)$, so the intersection over
the registered $\Nphi$ is $[1.917, 2.012)$, its lower end from $\Nphi = 64$ and
its upper from $\Nphi = 128$, containing $2$ and excluding $1$ and $3$. The harmonics: every registered Mach-Zehnder world
puts its two rows at a phase difference in $\{0, \Nphi/4, \Nphi/2\}$, where
$\mathcal K$ reads $C_\Sigma, 0, -C_\Sigma$ for every admissible $c_j$,
and the pair at the turn $0$ likewise, so those cells check the power
and nothing of the harmonics; only two slits test $\mathcal K$, and
the registered two-slit clicks under the wheel (L2b) do so at the fan's
grain (Table~\ref{tab:consequences}). The host map of record 156
\cite{log}, with the screen's fan, the birth wheel and the exact
phase, gives the bands $23.5$ pixels apart with a
correlation $0.963$ with the two-path cosine, the kernel of $j = 1$,
where the harmonic $j$ would give a fringe of period $23.5/j$ pixels
and the flat mixture a comb: consistent with $c_j = 0$ for $j \ne 1$, no
bound on a small admixture stated; the registered clicks under the
wheel correlate $0.891$ with the two-source cosine, as the weights do
($0.895$), so the kernel of $j = 1$ is what the run shows and no
admixture is bounded by it (Section~\ref{sec:nature}, row 2a). The
tables: the built click is this form to the tables' rounding, a record's
total over the $64$ birth phases from $65448/65536$ to $65773/65536$
(Part~III), the tables' violation of hypothesis (a), a part in $276$.

\paragraph{The limit.} As $\Nphi = 2^k$ grows the admissible readings are the
same family with $\Nphi/4$ constants: for two rows at the angle $\theta =
2\pi \Delta/\Nphi$, $\mathcal K(\theta) = \sum_{j\ \mathrm{odd}} c_j\cos(j\theta)$, the
closed cone of the even positive-definite functions on the circle with
odd harmonics only, whose extreme rays are the single cosines; the Born
kernel $\cos\theta$ is the first of them, and the visibility of two rows
is $\mathcal K(\theta)/\mathcal K(0)$. The limit adds nothing: no $\Nphi$ and no continuity
forces $c_j = [j = 1]$; the fundamental is what the register reads and
what the phase means (one step per declared rate; a detector at the
harmonic $j$ is a detector reading the wavelength $\Lambda/j$). Reached
for the form, the power and the multiplicity rule; not reached for the
harmonic constants; Tsirelson's value as a limit is Part~III's
Theorem~\ref{th:bell}. In the precedents' form of words: Sinha et al.'s
three-slit test read the third-order term $\kappa = 0.0064 \pm 0.0119$
\cite{sinha2010}; the click's form, a quadratic, gives $\kappa = 0$
exactly, and its power, pinned to $[1.917, 2.054)$ by the $\Nphi = 64$
cells and to $[1.917, 2.012)$ with the $\Nphi = 32$ and $128$ cells
registered since (record 363 \cite{log}), contains $2$
(Table~\ref{tab:nature}, row 2c); the harmonic constants $c_j$ are the
gap admitted.

\section{Quantum mechanics in the law: the founding formulas, one by one}\label{sec:qm}

How the law sees it, in one sentence: the state of a record is the
vector $f \in \Z[\Z_{\Nphi}]$ of the amounts that ended at each phase, which
is where the wavefunction sits; its evaluation at the $\Nphi$th roots of
unity is the discrete Fourier transform, which is where the
position--momentum duality sits; the click, the one comparison read out
per record, is where the measurement sits, and it is the only place the
law deletes. Nothing here claims that the law is quantum mechanics;
each founding formula is placed, with its status from the derivation
map \cite{derivations}, as reached, reached in form (on the law as
built, or under a hypothesis named beside it), a limit, or not
reached.

\paragraph{Planck, 1900: $E = hf$ \cite{planck1901}.} An identity of the
release. A lamp of quantum $h$ whose clock turns $s_h$ phase steps per
self-creation pays $hs_h$ per unit born; its frequency $f$ is $s_h/\Nphi$
cycles per self-creation, so $E = hs_h = (h\Nphi)f$, Planck's constant the
declared quantum times the circle (the derivation's 6.4). Reached; the
Bell lamps' $2$ per birth registered.

\paragraph{Einstein, 1905: the light quantum \cite{einstein1905q}.} The
row is the quantum: one unit of the free family, born with the content
$hs_h$, flying at $c$, landing whole at one cell of one click. Reached
as the law's definition of a message at its birth, not as a derivation:
after a re-emission the content and the turn part (a re-emitted row
keeps its content and takes the re-emitter's declared turn, the
derivation's 6.4), so Einstein's relation at the absorber, the energy
delivered equal to $hf$ of the light received, is not derived.

\paragraph{Bohr, 1913: $2\pi p r = jh$ \cite{bohr1913}.} Reached in
form from the turn under the action and the push (7.2), the deciding
reading unchecked: series H closes no orbit on the base, and the levels
and the Rydberg lines are not reached, the law having no energy of a
bound state (Table~\ref{tab:consequences}).

\paragraph{de Broglie, 1924: $\lambda = h/p$ \cite{debroglie1924}.} The
turn by momentum: a body's phase turns $|p|\Nphi/h$ per Link, a circle every
$h/|p|$ Links, so the wavelength is the declared turn (7.2); reached as
an identity under the declared key, the derivation's arithmetic on the
digital circle giving $2.041$ to $2.000$.

\paragraph{Heisenberg, 1925: the arrays between levels
\cite{heisenberg1925}; Born and Jordan, 1925: $pq - qp = h/(2\pi i)$
\cite{bornjordan1925}.} The first paper of quantum mechanics proper
replaced the orbit by arrays indexed by pairs of stationary states,
with the product rule and a quantum condition on their sums; the law
has no stationary states and no energy of a bound state, so that
structure is not reached. What is reached is the kinematics in Weyl's
form: the shift by one phase, $U$, and the multiplication by the
character $\zeta^y$ ($\zeta$ the primitive $\Nphi$th root, $y$ the phase),
$V$, on $\Z_{\Nphi}$ satisfy $VU = \zeta UV$ exactly (the derivation's 22.1,
the script's check to $10^{-15}$), the finite commutation relation of
position and momentum on the torus whose contraction as $\Nphi \to \infty$,
with $\hbar = h\Nphi/(2\pi)$, is $[x, p] = i\hbar$; and the click's weight
is one bilinear form $f^{\mathsf T}\mathbf G f$ with $\mathbf G$ the
Gram matrix of the tables (Section~\ref{sec:gleason}). This is the
finite quantum kinematics of Weyl \cite{weyl1931} and Schwinger
\cite{schwinger1960}, with $\Nphi$ a declared grain of the law rather than
a limit taken: reached in the Weyl form; Heisenberg's arrays between
levels not reached.

\paragraph{Schr\"odinger, 1926, for a free particle:
$i\hbar\,\partial_t\psi = -(\hbar^2/2m)\nabla^2\psi$
\cite{schrodinger1926}.} On the law as built, not reached: the rows'
limit is the wave equation at $c$, massless, $\omega = ck$ ($\omega$
the angular frequency, $k$ the wavenumber); a body's phase along its
path is de Broglie's, but a body is one record on one line, not a field
on the GameBoard, so there is no $\psi$ whose second difference the law
takes, and the massive dispersion is not in the flight table, where
every row flies at $c$. Under a hypothesis named and not built,
massive-rows-v1 (the derivation's 23), the time-independent free
equation is reached in form: a row carrying a momentum label
$\mathbf p$ and a content $M$ flies at the covariant pace $pc^2/E$ with
the wall $\Eint = \lfloor\sqrt{m^2 + 3\,\mathbf p\cdot\mathbf p}\rfloor$
(by comparisons at the birth, as $T_D$ is; no root in flight; the
photon its $m = 0$ case, today's $T_D$ to the integer) and
turns de Broglie's $|p_a|\Nphi/h$ per Link on the axis $a$, so its phase at
the Node $\mathbf x$ is $(\Nphi/h)\,\mathbf p\cdot(\mathbf x - \mathbf x_0)$
exactly, to the accumulator's remainder (the floor's one step at the
click), the plane wave on the Nodes; over the fan the record's phases
form the spherical wave, and the stationary limit of the block is the
Helmholtz equation $\nabla^2\psi + k^2\psi = 0$ with $k = 2\pi p/h$,
the time-independent Klein--Gordon form exactly (the dispersion the
exact square) and Schr\"odinger's at second order in $pc^2/E_0$, with
Born's $|\psi|^2$ the click's Gram form at the click and nowhere
between clicks. The time-dependent equation
is not reached: the row carries no frequency in flight, which the click
forces (two rows of one record arrive at different times and the click
compares the phases stamped at each arrival; a turn per interval would
put the phase velocity in place of de Broglie's wave number and erase
the fringes), and a record has one momentum, so no packet spreads. The
pin before any run is the two slits with massive rows,
\texttt{slits\_matter} (to be written): the bright bands at the pixels
$36.5$, $60$ and $83.5$ within one, from $\lambda = h/p = 4.6545$
Links, the same fringes as the photon world's from de Broglie's $h/p$
in place of $c\Nphi d/n$; Pearson $0.96 \pm 0.02$; the centre's first click
at the interval $1 + 828$ within $2$, the pace $pc^2/E$; the
confrontation the electron's two slits \cite{jonsson1961,tonomura1989}
in the same two-path law. The price is stated plainly: a massive
quantum in flight is a record of rows, born once as a record of rows
over the fan and not replicating in flight (no row re-emits, no release
per interval), and matter is a body after the click, the click the only
bridge (the derivation's 23.5; the owner's yes, record 332
\cite{log}); whether a bound
electron is the body of series H or a standing record of rows under a
potential is open, and the potential's form is named (the eikonal of
23.4) and not derived further.

\paragraph{Born, 1926: the rule \cite{born1926}.} Reached as a limit
and forced in form: a cell's probability is $(\rung_k - \rung_{k-1})/\Nphi$,
the difference of two cumulative rungs each within $1/(2\Nphi)$ of its
cumulative weight, so within $1/\Nphi$ of the Born weight $R_k/C_K$
($R_k$ the cell's weight, $C_K$ the total; $\rung_k/\Nphi$ is the
cumulative probability; a cell of weight below $1/(2\Nphi)$ can still
click, and three equal weights at $\Nphi = 64$ give the counts $21, 22,
21$, the middle one not the nearest integer to $64/3$;
Definition~\ref{def:click}; the derivation's 6.2), and the weight is a positive quadratic form of the
record forced by the rotation, the balanced splitter's conservation and
the counts alone, with the power $2$ pinned by the registered $63/1$
and $27, 5, 5, 27$ (the lattice Gleason, Theorem~\ref{th:gleason}); the
harmonic constants beyond $c_1$ are pinned to $c_1$ by a host map, not
by a run.

\paragraph{Heisenberg, 1927: $\Delta x\,\Delta p \ge \hbar/2$
\cite{heisenberg1927,kennard1927}.} Reached exactly on the circle in
its support and entropic forms and as the limit $\Nphi \to \infty$ in
Kennard's variance form, with the one read-out per record as the reason
it is an uncertainty and not a spread (Section~\ref{sec:gleason}; the
derivation's 22). The single opening's product is pinned before its run
at $0.92 \pm 0.03$ for the width $27$ (Section~\ref{sec:gleason});
Table~\ref{tab:nature}'s row 10 is not yet run under the one click, its
last registered value the crowd form's $1.08$.

\paragraph{Dirac, 1928: spin \cite{dirac1928}.} Not reached. A row
carries a hand, one pseudoscalar bit that the $48$ read by the
determinant (Theorem~\ref{th:group}) and the weak column reads as the
chirality of the decay; the law has no angular-momentum ledger and no
spin dynamics, no precession and no spin-$\tfrac12$ against spin-$1$
(\cite{beamlaw}, note 39).

\paragraph{Bell, 1964, and Tsirelson, 1980 \cite{bell1964,tsirelson1980}.}
Measured and proved: the CHSH value $S = 176/64 = 2.75$ at $\Nphi = 64$
with exact marginals ($32/64$ in all $4096$ setting pairs, no
signalling, Theorem~\ref{th:marginals}), $S(\Nphi, N_t) \to 2\sqrt2$ with the
bound of Theorem~\ref{th:bell}, the register's row 1a PASS at $1.65$
standard errors above \cite{hensen2015}. The rule is a deterministic
one, given the birth phase, and its value above $2$ is not a local
hidden-variable value: the rule weighs the two readings jointly, one
Gram form over both settings, quadratic in the record vector with cross
terms between its cells, and signals nothing (Part~III,
Section~\ref{sec:causal}). By Fine's theorem a joint distribution of
the four observables, two settings a side, returning the measured pair
distributions as its marginals exists exactly when the CHSH
inequalities hold \cite{fine1982}, so the registered $176/64$ has none
(the derivation's 22.3).

In sum: of the founding formulas, Planck's and de Broglie's are reached
as identities, Einstein's as the definition of a message at birth, the
Weyl form of the kinematics and the uncertainty relation exactly on the
circle with their continuum forms as limits, Born's rule as a limit
with its form forced, Bell's inequality violated and Tsirelson's bound
reached as a limit; Bohr's condition is reached in form; Schr\"odinger's
time-independent free equation is reached in form under a hypothesis
named and not built, its time-dependent form not; Heisenberg's arrays
between levels and Dirac's spin are not reached, and the place where
each would enter is named. The law's
quantum mechanics is a finite one, on $\Z_{\Nphi}$ with the grain declared,
whose continuum forms return as $\Nphi \to \infty$; where the two differ at
finite $\Nphi$ (the power's window, the CHSH value on both sides of
$2\sqrt2$, the visibility's second digit) is what a measurement can
refute (Section~\ref{sec:nature}). In the precedents' form of words:
Hensen et al.'s pair read $S = 2.42 \pm 0.20$
\cite{hensen2015}, and the law's $S = 2.75$ at $\Nphi = 64$ lies $1.65$
standard errors above it and $0.078$ below the bound
(Table~\ref{tab:nature}, row 1a); Bohr's Balmer ratio $27/20$ awaits
its line (row 6), the gap admitted.

\section{The formulas of flow, heat and temperature: what the GameBoard has of each}\label{sec:continuum}

The derivation's section 25 \cite{derivations} takes the big formulas
of the continuum, the ones that physics brought with it before any
quantum, and asks of each what the GameBoard has of it: the reading
that shows it (a detector's or the GameBoard's), its standing under the
six-point standard, its pin and its verdict; round 1 is the inventory
(Table~\ref{tab:continuum}), round 2 the derivations of what the
inventory finds reachable now (25.4 to 25.10: the continuity equation
exact from the books; Euler's form with the Galilean factor and the
sound speed in closed form; the one-axis Navier--Stokes with the shear
frozen; diffusion, Fick and Fourier as one tensor law; temperature as
a reading pinned on the thrown stars of series G2, with the ideal gas
law and equipartition's half as identities of readings; Newton's cooling as
the release's exponential, pinned on the Bell lamp's stall; temperature
from entropy not reached). Three facts of the law decide
most rows: rows in flight are ballistic on their lines (the flight
blind to the crowd, P9), so a fluid of many emitters' rows does not mix
by collision and only the six-heading gas of one number under the
collision table flows; bodies meet by contact and reflection, never by
an elastic exchange of momentum, so nothing thermalises; and the
bijection holds between clicks (Theorem~\ref{th:bijection}), so the
count of Table~\ref{tab:consequences}'s entropy row (the derivation's
section 14) changes only at the click. Of the twenty-three, by the
inventory's own tally: three are reached exactly (the continuity
equation from the books, Boltzmann's count, the second law at the
click, the last exact at the click and in form for the kinetic $H$); six in form on the six-heading gas
of one number (Euler and Navier--Stokes with the known obstacle of the
cubic lattice gas: on the six-Link GameBoard the fourth-rank momentum
flux tensor is not isotropic and a Galilean factor $g(\rho)$ appears, the cure of the hexagon \cite{fhp1986} and
the FCHC projection \cite{dhumieres1986} not available on six Ports;
Bernoulli; the diffusion equation and Fick's law as one tensor law,
the tensor exact at rational densities and anisotropic along the cube's
diagonal, since the collision table is not equivariant under the $48$
(round 1's isotropy withdrawn in round 2); Fourier's law on the
content; the sound speed in closed form from the table, $c/\sqrt3$ at
low density); three as
identities of readings (temperature as a reading, the mean squared
momentum per unit of content per axis; the ideal gas law per axis;
Newton's cooling of the content); two by reference (Maxwell's
equations, the wave equation); and nine not reached, of which four
fall to one identity named and not designed, elastic-contact-v1, the
one-dimensional elastic exchange of the axis components at a refused
step (rows 9, 10, 19 and 21), and the isotropic Navier--Stokes to
fan-collision-v1. Nothing enters the law; no run.

\section{The confrontation with nature}\label{sec:nature}

Only a detector's reading is a measurement; a number of the GameBoard
(a probe's presence, a row's count) is never compared with nature
except as a pin marked so, and the register's rules and the owner's
rule of the day say so \cite{nature,log}. The program keeps a
confrontation register \cite{nature}: one row per
registered detector reading that has a dimensionless counterpart in
nature, read under one parameter set (the grain and the family table as
the registered worlds declare them, nothing moved to meet an experiment;
nine rows' worlds declare another clock pair, flagged in the register,
and no verdict depends on it), one published source per value, one
verdict per row: PASS within the stated uncertainty, FAIL with the
number, BOUND where the comparison bounds a free parameter, NOT YET
where the reading is not registered. A FAIL is a result of the law and is
stated with the same care as a pass; where a derivation has pinned the
reading and its run is not made, the verdict is given from the pin and
says so, and the row is also a NOT YET for the run. The register's
twenty-two rows (its base and its restatements by commit in
Reproducibility; five
PASS (four re-run and checked by the second runner under record 353's rule, rows 1a, 2b, 2c and 9 \cite{replications}; row 11b a pin without its run), twelve FAIL, of which four are pinned and one is a refutation of a
declared input, one row (12, the clock's word, series T) PASS under the age word (the clock's word by the owner's assumption) and FAIL under the presence word (the alternative); two BOUND; two NOT YET), are
Table~\ref{tab:nature}; nine of the published values are marked in the
register as to be verified against their sources before submission
(the visibility of \cite{grangier1986}, the Sorkin parameter of
\cite{sinha2010}, the uncertainties of \cite{bailey1977},
\cite{botermann2014} and \cite{nagel2015}, the figure of
\cite{nairz2002}, the lifetime of \cite{gonzalez2021}, the exponents of
\cite{blondin2008} and \cite{lubin2001}) and are printed here as the
register prints them; the
dimensional constants, which need the dictionary's conversion fixed
once, are not in this pass. Two cells are this paper's additions to
the register, marked so in the table: the covariant readings of
Section~\ref{sec:lorentz} in rows 4a and 5b, which the register
predates. Row 2a's clicks under the wheel are the register's own
reading \cite{nature}.
